<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">void</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">    // Drop the abort listener once the wait is over, so a long streamed reply</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">    // doesn't pile up one closure per token on the signal.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ChatAppProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * A complete agent workspace: an AI sidebar, a conversation where each agent</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * turn reasons, lays out a plan, works through it and streams its reply, and a</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * prompt bar with stop. Ships with a scripted fake agent.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> ChatApp</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
